# Real proposals, labelling cards, shard 1 of 3

100 cards. Labeller name for this shard: shard-1.

Notes that apply to every card in this file:
- Any em-dash found in on-chain text is shown here as ' - ' (a space, a hyphen, a space). The record in sample.jsonl keeps the original character, written as a JSON escape.
- Whole-token figures divide the base units by 10 to the power of the token's decimals, with the token taken from the place named in the table. They assume the field is in the token's own base units, as it is for NEP-141 and NEP-245 transfers. For other contracts the field may use another scale, so read the call as shown.
- Decimals come from the census token list (the 1Click token list read on 20 Oct 2026) or are marked unknown. Nothing was looked up on chain for the cards.
- A card shows facts from the chain and the census. It carries no labels. The label is yours.
- A string longer than 3000 characters (for example a contract upload sent as base64) is cut in the display, with a marker that gives its full length. sample.jsonl holds it in full.
- Position and shard numbers refer to the order of eval/real/sample.jsonl. The shards take every third record: shard 1 holds records 1, 4, 7, shard 2 holds 2, 5, 8, shard 3 holds 3, 6, 9.

Description, exact text from chain (298 characters):
~~~~text
Allow $HOOT (hoot-8ecf65.launchpad.justhoot.near) as a quote token on launchpad.justhoot.near, so memes can launch paired with HOOT. HOOT has no oracle price, so it is priced from its 1% HOOT/wNEAR DCL pool (hoot-8ecf65.launchpad.justhoot.near|wrap.near|10000), converted at the oracle wNEAR price.
~~~~
